### [103] [Traversable Wormholes Supported by Conformally Coupled Scalar Fields in $D \geq 4$: Geometry, Geodesics, and Quasinormal Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38217)
*Pablo Vidal*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由无质量共形耦合标量场产生的双参数$(μ, b)$非对称可穿越虫洞解，分析了其几何性质、零测地线、标量扰动的准正规模谱，发现该虫洞与BBMB/极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞存在等谱性，并探讨了可穿越性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虫洞研究多集中于对称解，而非对称虫洞的性质尚未充分探索。本文旨在系统分析双参数非对称虫洞族的时空结构、能量条件违反、光子运动行为、扰动谱特性以及可穿越性，为理解奇异物质支撑的虫洞几何提供理论依据。

Method: 1) 分析双参数$(μ, b)$虫洞解的时空正则性和渐近平直性；2) 定义虫洞喉部并验证喇叭口条件，检验经典能量条件的违反；3) 通过有效势分析零测地线方程，数值计算光子轨迹并用嵌入图可视化；4) 求解无质量Klein-Gordon方程控制的标量扰动，半解析和数值方法计算准正规模(QNM)谱；5) 推导Morris-Thorne型可穿越性条件并计算穿越固有时间。

Result: 1) 在适当参数范围内，时空处处正则且具有两个渐近平直区域；2) 喇叭口条件的满足导致经典能量条件违反，需要奇异物质维持喉部开放；3) 成功计算并可视化了光子在虫洞中的运动轨迹；4) 标量扰动仅由参数$μ$控制，在$D=4$维时发现虫洞与BBMB/极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞之间存在等谱性；5) 获得了可穿越性的具体条件和穿越者的固有时间表达式。

Conclusion: 本文全面分析了双参数非对称可穿越虫洞的物理性质，揭示了其与极端黑洞的等谱关系，证明了标量扰动的单参数依赖性。研究表明该虫洞解需要奇异物质支撑，满足特定条件下可实现穿越。这些结果为虫洞物理学提供了新的理论洞察,并为未来的扩展研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this work, we analyze a two-parameter $(μ, b)$ asymmetric family of traversable wormhole solutions generated by a massless conformally coupled scalar field. We show that, for a suitable range of parameters, the spacetime is everywhere regular and has two asymptotically flat regions. We then define the wormhole throat and verify the flare-out condition, which leads to violations of the classical energy conditions in GR, thereby requiring exotic matter to hold the throat open. We further analyze the motion of photons governed by null geodesics through an effective potential. The resulting trajectories are computed numerically and visualized using embedding diagrams. Following this route, we investigate scalar perturbations governed by the massless Klein-Gordon equation and find the QNM spectrum semianalytically and numerically. Interestingly enough, we show that such perturbations are governed by only one parameter $μ$. Therefore, in $D = 4$, we find an isospectrality between the wormhole and the BBMB/Extremal Reissner-Nordström black hole. In the spirit of the seminal work of Morris and Thorne, we derive the conditions for traversability and compute the proper time for a traveler to traverse the wormhole. Finally, we discuss possible extensions and future directions for our work.

</details>


### [104] [A Strong Cosmologically Coupled Mass Growth Solution in Linearized General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38303)
*Kevin S. Croker,Joshua Cohon,Viktor T. Toth,Massimiliano Rinaldi,Nicolas Fernandez,Joel L. Weiner,Duncan Farrah,Gregory Tarlé,Gustavo Niz,Christopher Cain,James W. Rohlf,Alejandro Aviles,Jake Summers,Leander Thiele,Rogier A. Windhorst,Joshua Grumski-Flores*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个广义相对论的线性阶解，其中黑洞质量随宇宙膨胀按 a^κ 被动增长，为宇宙学耦合黑洞(CCBH)作为宇宙加速膨胀的解释提供了理论基础，并在 κ=3 时得到能量化真空云，使得通过脉冲星双星系统进行观测验证成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI数据发布2加剧了人们对宇宙学耦合黑洞(CCBHs)作为解释宇宙晚期加速膨胀现象的兴趣，但已知的CCBH模型增长速度太慢，无法驱动这种加速膨胀。因此需要构建一个新的理论模型，使黑洞质量能够以足够快的速率随宇宙膨胀而增长(目标为 κ~3)。

Method: 在线性阶近似下构建广义相对论的精确解，该解的Hernandez-Misner-Sharp质量随宇宙标度因子按 a^κ 规律被动增长。常数 κ 进入Weyl算符和应力张量算符的所有本征值，确保增长在自由场和物质扇区中都具有明确的物理意义。对解进行因果性验证，确保其在与恒星坍缩致密天体相关的时空范围内满足因果律。

Result: 成功构建了质量按 a^κ 增长的广义相对论解，该解在恒星坍缩致密天体相关的时空区域内满足因果性。当 κ=3 时，涌现出能量化真空云结构，这与其他研究中预测的能量化真空黑洞质量按 a³ 标度的结果相吻合。这是向 κ~3 的CCBH精确解迈出的第一步。

Conclusion: 本研究为构建能够驱动宇宙加速膨胀的宇宙学耦合黑洞模型提供了重要的理论进展。通过线性阶解的构建和 κ=3 时能量化真空云的涌现，为理解CCBHs作为暗能量候选者奠定了基础。该理论框架使得利用脉冲星双星系统进行观测检验成为可能,为验证CCBHs假说提供了实际可行的观测路径。

Abstract: DESI Data Release 2 has heightened interest in cosmologically coupled black holes (CCBHs) as an explanation for accelerating late-time expansion, but known models for CCBHs grow too slowly to drive it. We construct at linear order a solution to General Relativity whose Hernandez-Misner-Sharp mass passively grows $\propto a^κ$ as the universe expands. The constant $κ$ enters all eigenvalues of the Weyl and stress-tensor operators, so growth is unambiguously physical in both free-field and material sectors. The solution is causal over spacetime relevant for stellar collapse compact objects. A cloud of energized vacuum emerges at $κ= 3$, complementing the $a^3$ scaling predicted elsewhere for the masses of energized vacuum black holes. This first step toward $κ\sim 3$ CCBH exact solutions enables observational tests with pulsar binaries.

</details>


### [105] [Towards solving General Relativity with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38331)
*Juan A. Carretero,Jorge F. Urbán,Fernando Abalos,Joan Massó,Oscar A. Reula,Jose A. Pons,Carlos Palenzuela*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究将物理信息神经网络(PINNs)应用于爱因斯坦广义相对论方程的求解，通过标准数值相对论基准测试验证了该方法的有效性，并成功模拟了孤立玻色子星的动力学演化，证明PINNs是求解物质-爱因斯坦耦合方程的可行补充框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值相对论方法依赖于预定义的计算网格，存在网格依赖性和计算复杂度等限制。PINNs作为一种新兴的机器学习框架，能够通过约束神经网络满足物理定律来近似偏微分方程组的解，生成不依赖网格的连续表示，可在训练域内任意点求值。本文旨在探索PINNs在广义相对论爱因斯坦方程求解中的应用潜力，为数值相对论提供替代性求解框架。

Method: 本文采用物理信息神经网络(PINNs)框架求解爱因斯坦场方程。具体方法包括：(1)建立适用于广义相对论的数学表述和网络架构；(2)设计包含物理约束的损失函数，确保神经网络满足爱因斯坦方程；(3)制定有效的训练策略以获得精确的时空解；(4)在三维笛卡尔坐标系下直接操作，模拟孤立玻色子星的动力学演化，通过标准数值相对论基准测试验证方法的准确性。

Result: 研究通过一系列标准数值相对论基准测试成功验证了PINNs方法的有效性。在天体物理应用中，该方法成功再现了高度致密玻色子星的平衡结构，并准确恢复了其特征径向振荡频率。模拟结果表明，在三维笛卡尔坐标系中直接运行的PINN能够有效处理孤立孤子型玻色子星的动力学演化问题，展现出与传统数值方法相当的求解能力。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了物理信息神经网络(PINNs)作为求解耦合物质-爱因斯坦方程的可行补充框架。PINNs能够成功应用于广义相对论爱因斯坦方程的求解，在标准基准测试和实际天体物理场景(如玻色子星演化)中均表现出良好性能。该方法为数值相对论提供了一种无需预定义网格、可在连续域内求值的替代求解途径,为探索数值相对论的新表述和计算方法开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are a machine-learning framework for approximating solutions to systems of partial differential equations by constraining neural networks to satisfy the underlying physical laws. The resulting continuous representation does not require a predefined computational mesh and can be evaluated at arbitrary points within the training domain. In this work, we investigate the application of PINNs to Einstein's equations of General Relativity. We discuss the mathematical formulation, network architectures, loss functions, and training strategies used to obtain accurate spacetime solutions, and demonstrate the approach through a series of standard benchmarks from Numerical Relativity. We then extend the method to a more astrophysical setting by considering the dynamical evolution of an isolated solitonic boson star. Our simulations show that a PINN operating directly in three-dimensional Cartesian coordinates can reproduce the equilibrium structure of a highly compact boson star and recover its characteristic radial oscillation frequencies. These results establish PINNs as a viable complementary framework for solving coupled matter-Einstein equations and exploring alternative formulations in numerical relativity.

</details>


### [106] [Explicit Analytical Representations for the Schwarzschild Radial Equation via Hypergeometric and Frobenius Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38461)
*J. G. R. Valangelis,Ailton C. Nascimento,Helder A. S. Costa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文严格推导了Schwarzschild黑洞外部大质量Klein-Gordon方程的精确约化为confluent-Heun型径向微分方程,揭示了次主导离心项对附属参数的关键修正,并通过物理坐标变换和Frobenius级数构造了高精度的视界归一化解,阐明了准正则模谱选择本质上是不规则奇点处的连接问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schwarzschild黑洞背景下的大质量Klein-Gordon方程约化为confluent-Heun型方程时,传统处理往往忽略次主导离心项,导致附属参数不精确。同时,物理紧化坐标下超几何展开退化为五项递推关系,无法简化为三项形式,这给准确求解和准正则模频谱分析带来了技术障碍。需要建立严格的约化方案和高效的数值求解方法。

Method: 1) 严格保留次主导离心项进行精确约化,保持视界指标指数不变但关键性地修正附属参数;2) 在Svartholm-Schmidt超几何展开框架下推导三项递推关系,建立修正的连分数相容性条件,将最小尾部与低端递推匹配;3) 证明物理紧化坐标z=(r-1)/r将不规则奇点移至z=1,使超几何展开固有地转化为无法退化的五项递推;4) 为绕过此障碍,通过z=0处的直接Frobenius级数构造视界归一化物理分支,获得高阶收敛性和近精度极限的残差。

Result: 构造的Frobenius级数解在与精确径向常微分方程对比时表现出高阶收敛性和接近精度下限的残差,验证了方法的有效性。明确揭示了物理坐标变换导致的五项递推结构及其不可约化性,成功通过直接级数方法获得了高精度的视界归一化物理解。

Conclusion: 本文通过严格保留次主导离心项实现了Schwarzschild黑洞上大质量Klein-Gordon方程向confluent-Heun型方程的精确约化,揭示了附属参数的关键修正。证明了物理紧化坐标固有地产生无法简化的五项递推障碍,并通过Frobenius级数方法成功构造了高精度的视界归一化解。研究阐明了准正则模频谱选择本质上是不规则奇点处的连接问题,为黑洞扰动理论的精确分析提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: The massive Klein-Gordon equation on the Schwarzschild exterior reduces to a radial differential equation of confluent-Heun type. We present its exact reduction by rigorously retaining a subleading centrifugal term that preserves the horizon indicial exponents but critically shifts the accessory parameters. Within the Svartholm--Schmidt hypergeometric-expansion framework, we derive the three-term recurrence relation and establish the corrected continued-fraction compatibility condition matching the minimal tail to the lower-end recurrence. Crucially, we demonstrate that the physical compactification coordinate $z=(r-1)/r$ shifts the irregular singular point to $z=1$, inherently transforming the hypergeometric expansion into a five-term recurrence that cannot degenerate to three terms. To circumvent this obstruction, we construct the horizon-normalized physical branch via a direct Frobenius series at $z=0$. This representation exhibits high-order convergence and near-precision-floor residuals against the exact radial ODE, while clarifying why quasinormal-mode spectral selection remains a connection problem at the irregular singular endpoint.

</details>


### [107] [Non-Metricity Trace Forms and Weak-Field Light Deflection: A Geometric Optical Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38568)

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了引力光偏转的弱场非度量操作框架，通过非度量性迹形式直接推导有效折射率，并从宏观膨胀源出发得到爱丁顿偏转角，无需假设微观物质标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统广义相对论中光偏转的度量描述依赖于物质标度的微观假设（杆和钟的变形），本文旨在发展一种基于非度量性的操作性框架，将光学响应直接关联到几何的迹部分，而不引入外部光学类比，从而建立从物质源到光偏转的自洽弱场理论链。

Method: 在Schwarzschild弱场的各向同性表述下，显式计算非度量性迹1-形式W和P，通过精确关系式d ln n = W - 4P建立有效折射率n = g/f与非度量性迹扇区的直接联系；引入有效标量校准势χ，由观测者投影的宏观膨胀流驱动，在静态弱场极限下满足∇²χ = -κρ；通过点质量校准固定Φ = χ/C₀ ≈ 2GM/(rc²)，执行光学计算得到偏转角。

Result: 计算得到经典的爱丁顿光偏转角4GM/(bc²)，其中b为冲击参数。关键在于建立了完整的弱场理论链：从宏观膨胀源→非度量性迹→有效折射率→光偏转，该结果的意义不在于修正数值预测，而在于提供了无需微观物质变形假设的理论路径。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了引力光偏转的弱场非度量操作性理论，证明光传播的有效折射率不是外部引入的光学类比，而是直接由非度量性的迹扇区决定。通过宏观膨胀流驱动校准势的方案，在不依赖微观标度律假设的情况下，建立了从物质分布到光偏转的闭合理论链，为引力的几何-光学解释提供了新的基础性视角。

Abstract: This paper develops a weak-field, non-metric operational account of gravitational light deflection. In the isotropic formulation of the Schwarzschild weak field, the non-metricity trace 1-forms W and P are computed explicitly and shown to determine the optical response through the exact relation $d\ln n=W-4P$, $n=g/f$. Thus the effective refractive index governing null propagation is not introduced as an external optical analogy, but is tied directly to the trace sector of non-metricity. To connect the trace geometry with matter, an effective scalar calibration potential $χ$ is sourced by an observer-projected macroscopic dilation current, yielding $\nabla^2χ=-κρ$ in the static weak-field limit. The point-mass calibration fixes $Φ=χ/C_0\simeq 2GM/(rc^2)$, and the resulting optical calculation gives the Eddington deflection angle $4GM/(bc^2)$. The significance of the result is not a modified numerical prediction, but a closed weak-field chain from macroscopic dilation sourcing to non-metricity traces, effective refractive index, and light deflection, without postulating a microscopic deformation law for material rods or clocks.

</details>

Abstract: In this note we present a direct relation between the geometrical definition of a quasi-Einstein metric and Horava gravity: the solutions of the large-distance effective action of Horava gravity that are static and orthogonal are quasi-Einstein metrics. The converse is also true if the function over the quasi-Einstein manifold satisfies a given equation.

</details>


### [109] [Quadrupolar logarithmic tidal response of Einstein Euler Heisenberg black holes: the role of the second electromagnetic invariant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38649)
*Ramon Becar,P. A. Gonzalez,Ali Ovgun,Joel Saavedra,Yerko Vasquez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究带电爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡黑洞的四极对数潮汐响应，通过耦合静态奇宇称引力和电磁方程，在欧拉-海森堡耦合的一阶近似下，推导出规范运行矩阵，并分析了四次不变量对轴向涨落的贡献及其在极端限制下的有效场论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是确定带电爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡黑洞的四极对数潮汐响应特性。由于电磁背景下第二电磁不变量消失，但其二次相互作用仍对轴向涨落方程有贡献，因此需要通过耦合的静态奇宇称引力和电磁方程来深入分析这一物理过程，特别是在非线性电动力学框架下研究黑洞的潮汐形变响应。

Method: 采用直接渐近递推方法，从耦合的静态奇宇称引力和电磁方程出发，在欧拉-海森堡耦合参数a的一阶近似下进行计算。具体方法包括：(1)分析两个四次不变量在非零通道中的部分抵消效应；(2)使用作用量归一化场使引力电磁互易性显式化；(3)对规范运行矩阵进行局域极端极限分析，并与有效场论结果进行约定转换后的对比；(4)通过允许独立的四次耦合来研究相同电背景几何下不同轴向运行矩阵的情况。

Result: 在欧拉-海森堡耦合的一阶近似下，得到规范运行矩阵C_can^EH=(9aQ²/10)·[[0,-Q],[-Q,M]]，该矩阵定义为r^(-2)log(r₀/r)项中规范源向量的系数。局域系数在该微扰阶完全保留了对次极端比Q/M的依赖关系。两个四次不变量在两个非零通道中部分抵消，作用量归一化场使引力电磁互易性变得显式。运行矩阵的局域极端极限在约定转换后与相应的双不变量有效场论结果一致。研究还表明，相同的电背景几何可以具有不同的轴向运行矩阵，并在指定的双算符模型内给出了代数逆关系。

Conclusion: 本文成功确定了带电爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡黑洞在最小耦合四次电动力学框架内的局域对数运行特性。计算表明，虽然电磁背景下第二不变量消失，但其二次相互作用对轴向涨落仍有重要贡献。规范运行矩阵在微扰一阶完整保留了对电荷-质量比的依赖，且在极端极限下与有效场论预测一致。研究进一步揭示了背景几何与轴向响应的非平凡关联。需要注意的是，有限的视界匹配响应还需要额外的全局匹配计算才能完整确定。

Abstract: We determine the quadrupolar logarithmic tidal response of electrically charged Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg black holes from the coupled static odd-parity gravitational and electromagnetic equations. Although the second electromagnetic invariant vanishes on the electric background, its quadratic interaction contributes to the axial fluctuation equations. At first order in the Euler-Heisenberg coupling $a$, a direct asymptotic recursion gives the canonical running matrix $C_{\rm can}^{\rm EH}=(9aQ^2/10)\left(\begin{smallmatrix}0&-Q\\-Q&M\end{smallmatrix}\right)$, deffined as the coefficients multiplying the canonical source vector in the $r^{-2}\log(r_0/r)$ term. The resulting local coefficients retain the full dependence on the the subextremal ratio $\frac{Q}{M}$ at this perturbative order. The two quartic invariants partially cancel in both nonzero channels, while the action-normalized fields make gravitoelectromagnetic reciprocity explicit. The local extremal limit of the running matrix reproduces the corresponding two-invariant effective-field-theory result after convention conversion. Allowing independent quartic couplings further shows that identical electric background geometries can have different axial running matrices and yields algebraic inverse relations within the specified two-operator model. The calculation determines local logarithmic running within minimally coupled quartic electrodynamics; the finite horizon-matched response requires an additional global matching calculation.

</details>


### [110] [RRR fluctuations from non-singular bounce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38990)
*Sayantan Choudhury*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在包含收缩相、非奇异反弹和慢滚到超慢滚急剧转变的暴胀框架中，系统处理了正规化、重整化和重求和问题，计算了功率谱的量子圈修正，消除了紫外发散和红外发散，证明了方案独立性，并展示了如何在最小暴胀条件下产生太阳质量级原初黑洞，同时满足因果性、幺正性和微引力透镜观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暴胀模型在产生原初黑洞(PBH)时面临"无解定理"的限制，难以在保持因果性和幺正性的同时，用最小暴胀e-folding数生成太阳质量量级的PBH。本文旨在通过引入收缩-反弹-暴胀的完整宇宙学框架，并结合有效场论方法系统处理量子修正中的紫外和红外发散问题，突破现有理论限制，为PBH形成提供自洽的理论描述。

Method: 采用有效场论框架，在包含收缩相、非奇异反弹和暴胀(从慢滚到超慢滚急剧转变)的完整演化过程中计算功率谱的量子圈修正。通过重整化技术消除二次紫外发散，利用重求和方法软化对数红外发散，并验证结果的重整化方案独立性。系统分析声速参数$c_s$在0.88到1范围内对功率谱振幅的影响，确保理论满足因果性和幺正性约束，并将不同质量区间的PBH丰度与微引力透镜观测数据进行对比验证。

Result: 在总暴胀e-folding数$ΔN_{\rm Total}\sim{\cal O}(60)$的最小暴胀条件下，成功产生太阳质量量级($M_{\rm PBH}\sim{\cal O}(M_\odot)$)的原初黑洞，规避了无解定理。当声速满足$0.88\le c_s\le1$时，功率谱峰值振幅控制在$10^{-3}\le A\le10^{-2}$范围内，保持因果性和幺正性。量子修正的紫外和红外发散得到有效处理，证实了方案独立性。PBH在$10^{-33}\!-\!10^{-27}M_\odot$和$10^{-6}\!-\!10^{-1}M_\odot$质量区间的丰度预测与微引力透镜观测约束一致，避免了PBH过度产生问题。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个自洽的理论框架，通过引入收缩-反弹-暴胀完整演化过程，结合系统的量子修正处理(正规化、重整化、重求和)，成功解决了原初黑洞产生理论中的关键难题。证明了在最小暴胀条件下生成太阳质量级PBH的可行性，突破了传统无解定理的限制，同时确保理论满足基本的物理一致性条件(因果性、幺正性、方案独立性)和观测约束(微引力透镜数据)，为PBH作为暗物质候选者提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We present a consistent treatment of regularization, renormalization, and resummation in a framework involving a contracting phase, a non-singular bounce, and inflation with a sharp transition from slow-roll to ultra-slow-roll, enabling primordial black hole production. Within an effective field theory approach, we compute quantum loop corrections to the power spectrum across all phases. Quadratic UV divergences are removed through renormalization, while logarithmic IR divergences are softened via resummation, demonstrating scheme independence. The inclusion of contraction and bounce allows the generation of solar-mass PBHs, $M_{\rm PBH}\sim{\cal O}(M_\odot)$, with minimal inflation $ΔN_{\rm Total}\sim{\cal O}(60)$, thereby evading the no-go theorem. For $0.88\le c_s\le1$, the peak spectrum amplitude lies within $10^{-3}\le A\le10^{-2}$, preserving causality and unitarity. PBH abundances across $10^{-33}\!-\!10^{-27}$ and $10^{-6}\!-\!10^{-1}M_\odot$ are confronted with microlensing constraints to control PBH overproduction.

</details>


### [111] [Primordial Tensor Signatures and Gravitational Wave Constraints in Lorentz Violating Inflation with Non-Canonical Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39087)
*Dibash Plaban Kalita,Shreya Banerjee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在洛伦兹破缺暴胀框架下具有非正则标量场动力学的原初张量扰动，通过引入Einstein-Æther型高阶动能项，推导了张量传播速度及其与洛伦兹破缺耦合的依赖关系，并利用张量谱指数和跑动建立了新的一致性关系，为通过原初引力波谱探测洛伦兹破缺提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暴胀理论通常基于洛伦兹不变性假设，但量子引力理论暗示洛伦兹对称性可能在高能标下破缺。同时，非正则标量场动力学（如包含高阶动能项）在暴胀模型中具有重要物理意义。本文旨在将洛伦兹破缺效应与非正则暴胀动力学结合，探索原初张量扰动（引力波）如何作为探测这些基本物理机制的独特观测窗口，并建立区别于标准暴胀的观测特征。

Method: 1. 采用Einstein-Æther型理论框架，在作用量中引入高阶动能项K_3X²；2. 推导包含洛伦兹破缺效应的张量扰动传播速度及其演化方程；3. 选取无势能背景解（potential free background）以实现解析求解；4. 考察两种代表性的洛伦兹破缺耦合形式：q_k=λ_Tφ_k²和q_k=λ_Tφ_k⁴；5. 计算相应的张量谱指数n_T及其跑动α_T，建立新的一致性关系；6. 将理论预言与GW170817/GRB170817A观测得到的引力波速度约束进行比较，分析从晚期宇宙约束推导原初约束时所需的假设条件。

Result: 对于两种耦合形式（q_k=λ_Tφ_k²和q_k=λ_Tφ_k⁴），张量谱指数n_T和跑动α_T满足不同于标准暴胀的一致性关系，这些关系携带洛伦兹破缺的独特印记。研究得到了来自张量倾斜度和跑动的理论约束，并与GW170817/GRB170817A事件对引力波速度的严格晚期约束进行了对比。结果表明，将晚期观测约束转化为原初时期约束需要对张量扇区耦合从暴胀到晚期宇宙的演化做出额外假设，这强调了直接从原初观测量约束的重要性。

Conclusion: 原初张量观测量（特别是张量谱指数及其跑动）可以作为探测洛伦兹破缺的互补探针，并能有效揭示其与非正则暴胀动力学的相互作用。不同的洛伦兹破缺耦合形式导致可观测的张量谱特征差异，为未来原初引力波观测（如CMB B模式极化、空间引力波探测器）提供了区分理论模型的判据。研究强调了原初时期约束相对于外推晚期约束的独立价值，为理解早期宇宙的基本对称性结构开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate primordial tensor perturbations in a Lorentz violating inflationary framework with non-canonical scalar field dynamics. Using an Einstein-Æther type with a higher order kinetic term $K_3X^2$, we derive the tensor propagation speed and its dependence on an effective Lorentz violating coupling. We consider an analytically tractable potential free background and study two representative couplings, $q_k=λ_Tφ_k^2$ and $q_k=λ_Tφ_k^4$. The resulting tensor spectral index $n_T$ and its running $α_T$ satisfy distinct consistency relations, providing potential signatures of Lorentz violation in the primordial gravitational wave spectrum. We derive corresponding constraints from the tensor tilt and running and compare them with the stringent late-time bound on the gravitational wave speed from GW170817/GRB170817A. We emphasize that translating this bound into primordial constraint requires an assumption about the evolution of tensor sector coupling between inflation and the late Universe. Our results demonstrate that primordial tensor observables can provide a complementary probe of Lorentz violation and its interplay with non-canonical inflationary dynamics.

</details>


### [112] [Second-order multipole response of two nearby incoherent sources in wave-optical imaging of a Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39110)
*Wenfu Cao,Hongsheng Zhang,Zong-Kuan Guo,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个相互非相干点源通过史瓦西黑洞进行波动光学成像的问题，利用球谐加法定理和有限孔径傅里叶变换构建了成像模型，发现双星系统的可分辨性本质上具有色散特性，并确定了二阶近似有效的波长范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞引力透镜对近距双星系统的波动光学成像特性，特别是如何从单一亮度中心源的参考图像中分离出双星结构，以及这种可分辨性如何依赖于波长、源间距和亮度比等参数。这对于理解黑洞成像中的精细结构探测具有重要意义。

Method: 采用球谐加法定理构建任意源方向的分波场；通过有限孔径傅里叶变换形成图像；将双星图像与位于亮度质心处、总亮度相等的单一源图像进行比较，以分离双星结构；围绕质心进行展开以消除一阶项，使结构修正由二阶中心矩μ₂=4a²q/(1+q)²控制；通过数值计算研究残差对源半间距、亮度比和波长的依赖关系。

Result: 数值计算证实了残差对源半间距和亮度比的预期依赖关系。在长波长下，成像核在源间距范围内变化很小，双星几乎无法与质心参考区分。随着波长减小，更精细的条纹增强了残差，在3≤Mω≤12(2.09≥λ/M≥0.52)范围内残差增长约三倍，尽管此时双星在瑞利意义上仍未分辨。二阶近似在17<Mω<18(0.349<λ/M<0.370)时相对误差首次达到1，表明需要更高阶源矩。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，近距双星系统的可分辨性本质上是色散的(波长依赖的)，并确定了双星既可通过残差探测又能被二阶近似准确描述的波长范围。这为黑洞引力透镜成像中的精细结构探测和多源系统分析提供了理论基础，表明即使在传统瑞利判据下无法分辨的情况下，波动光学效应仍可提供结构信息。

Abstract: We study wave-optical imaging of two nearby, mutually incoherent point sources by a Schwarzschild black hole. Using the spherical-harmonic addition theorem, we construct partial-wave fields for arbitrary source directions and form images through a finite-aperture Fourier transform. To isolate the binary structure, we compare the binary image with that of a single source of equal total brightness placed at the brightness centroid. Expanding about the centroid removes the first-order term exactly, so the leading structural correction is controlled by the second central moment, $μ_2=4a^2q/(1+q)^2$. Numerical calculations confirm the expected dependence of the residual on source half-separation and brightness ratio. We then study its wavelength dependence. At long wavelengths, the imaging kernel varies little across the source separation, making the binary nearly indistinguishable from the centroid reference. As the wavelength decreases, finer fringes enhance the residual, which grows by about a factor of three over $3\leq Mω\leq12$ ($2.09\geqλ/M\geq0.52$), even though the pair remains unresolved in the Rayleigh sense. Meanwhile, the second-order approximation gradually breaks down: its relative error first reaches unity for $17<Mω<18$ ($0.349<λ/M<0.370$), indicating the need for higher-order source moments. These results demonstrate that the distinguishability of a nearby binary is intrinsically chromatic and identify the wavelength range in which the binary is both detectable through its residual and accurately described at second order.

</details>


### [113] [Black Holes With Complete Asymptotic Throats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39186)
*Yi-Bo Liang,Hong-Rong Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究球对称黑洞在满足标准能量条件下,能否在有限非零面积半径处具有完备的内部端点,证明了完备渐近喉部与外部能量条件的相容性,并区分了完备性与不同层次的曲率正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典广义相对论中,黑洞内部结构通常以奇点终结。本文旨在探索一种替代可能性:黑洞是否可以在有限的非零面积半径处拥有完备的内部端点(渐近喉部结构),同时在视界外足够远的区域仍然满足标准能量条件。这涉及黑洞内部几何结构的根本性问题以及能量条件对时空完备性的约束。

Method: 保留两个独立的度规函数,分别处理类零型和类空型渐近喉部;推导类光测地线完备性的充要积分判据;在独立曲率分量的有界性和有限极限假设下,分析不同类型端点的曲率性质;获得时间型和类光平行传播标架下有界曲率的判据;通过显式实解析构造和光滑构造,验证渐近喉部结构与外部能量条件的相容性;证明径向零能量条件在完备内部端点附近必然失效的必然性定理。

Result: 类零型端点的极限曲率由喉部半径固定,类空型端点允许额外的非负曲率参数;完备类空型例子可以具有有界标量不变量和有界时间型标架曲率,但在无穷仿射参数处具有无界的类光标架曲率;径向零能量条件必须在每个完备内部端点附近任意接近处失效;构造了显式实解析解,在足够远的外部区域满足零、弱、主导和强能量条件;光滑构造可以在有限半径之外精确地变为史瓦西时空。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了完备渐近喉部与外部能量条件的相容性,表明球对称黑洞可以在有限非零面积半径处具有完备的内部端点,同时在视界外满足标准能量条件。研究明确区分了测地完备性与不同层次的曲率正则性(标量不变量有界、时间型标架曲率有界、类光标架曲率有界),揭示了黑洞内部几何结构的新可能性,为避免奇点的黑洞模型提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate whether a spherically symmetric black hole can have a complete inner end at finite, nonzero areal radius while satisfying the standard energy conditions sufficiently far outside its horizon. Retaining two independent metric functions, we treat both null-type and spacelike-type asymptotic throats and derive a necessary and sufficient integral criterion for causal geodesic completeness. Under boundedness and finite-limit assumptions on the independent curvature components, null-type ends have limiting curvature fixed by the throat radius, whereas spacelike-type ends admit an additional nonnegative curvature parameter. We obtain criteria for bounded curvature in timelike and null parallel-propagated frames. Complete spacelike-type examples can have bounded scalar invariants and bounded timelike-frame curvature but unbounded null-frame curvature at infinite affine parameter. We also prove that the radial null energy condition must fail arbitrarily close to every complete inner end in the stated class. Nevertheless, explicit real-analytic constructions with either type of throat satisfy the null, weak, dominant, and strong energy conditions throughout a sufficiently distant exterior region. Smooth constructions can become exactly Schwarzschild beyond a finite radius. These results establish the compatibility of complete asymptotic throats with exterior energy conditions while distinguishing completeness from different levels of curvature regularity.

</details>


### [114] [Nonlinear scalarization and thermodynamics in Einstein-Phantom scalar-Gauss-Bonnet theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39236)
*Tian-Zhen Dai,Yun Soo Myung,De-Cheng Zou,Meng-Yun Lai,Hyat Huang,Wen-Cong Gan,Lina Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了与高斯-博内不变量耦合的幻影标量场对施瓦西黑洞的非线性标量化。研究发现存在低分支和主分支两种解,低分支具有更高的Wald熵和更低的亥姆霍兹自由能,但其热容为负,表明低分支更稳定。最终证明幻影动力学理论中不存在非线性稳定的标量相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究幻影标量场与高斯-博内不变量耦合时对施瓦西黑洞标量化的影响,特别是研究非线性耦合依赖性及其热力学性质和稳定性。由于幻影因子在探测标量方程中消失,需要系统研究其非线性演化行为以及不同分支解的物理特性。

Method: 引入三种满足ζ''(0)=0条件的多项式耦合函数来研究非线性耦合依赖性。首先分析探测标量方程,发现幻影因子ε消失,使得非线性演化与正则标量理论相同。然后构造无节点探测解,并通过完全反作用计算得到低分支和主分支两类解。对两种分支在等质量和等温条件下进行热力学量(Wald熵、亥姆霍兹自由能、热容)的比较分析,最后研究非线性稳定性。

Result: 成功构造了无节点探测解和完全反作用的两个分支(低分支和主分支)。在等质量和等温条件下的比较显示:低分支具有更高的Wald熵和更低的亥姆霍兹自由能,表明其热力学上更优越;但低分支的热容为负值,这在热力学上意味着某种不稳定性。通过稳定性分析证明,在幻影动力学理论框架下不存在非线性稳定的标量相。

Conclusion: 研究表明幻影标量场与高斯-博内不变量耦合时,虽然低分支解在热力学上(更高的Wald熵和更低的自由能)比主分支更有利,但负热容揭示了其潜在的热力学不稳定性。最重要的结论是,幻影动力学理论中不存在非线性稳定的标量相,这为理解黑洞标量化及幻影场理论的稳定性提供了重要的理论依据,表明幻影标量场的这种耦合机制无法产生稳定的黑洞标量化配置。

Abstract: We study nonlinear scalarization of Schwarzschild black-holes for a phantom scalar coupled to the Gauss--Bonnet invariant. Three polynomial couplings satisfying $ζ''(0)=0$ are introduced to investigate its nonlinear coupling dependence. Since the phantom factor $ε$ disappears in the probe scalar equation, its nonlinear evolution is the same as in the canonical scalar theory. We construct nodeless probe solutions and fully backreacted two branches of lower and primary. At equal mass and equal temperature, respectively, the lower branches have higher Wald entropy and lower Helmholtz free energy than primary branch, but their heat capacities are negative. This implies that lower branch favors than primary branch. Finally, we show that there is no nonlinerly stable scalar phase for a phantom kinetic theory.

</details>


### [115] [Analysis of Phantom Cosmology With Different Potentials: Inflation and Isotropization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39281)
*Behzad Tajahmad,Hossein Kheiri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Bianchi I、Bianchi III和Kantowski-Sachs背景下,具有五类势函数的幽灵场模型中的暴涨动力学和各向同性化过程,通过动力系统方法识别并分析了87个临界点,扩展了Heusler命题,并重新审视了Collins-Hawking和Burd-Barrow的经典结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的暴涨宇宙学研究主要集中在标量场模型和各向同性背景,而对于幽灵场(phantom field)在各向异性时空中的暴涨动力学和各向同性化机制缺乏系统性研究。Heusler命题在处理某些情况时存在局限性,需要更强大的分析工具。此外,Collins-Hawking和Burd-Barrow关于各向异性宇宙演化的经典结论需要在幽灵场框架下重新检验,以探讨其普适性和深层概念意义。

Method: 本文采用动力系统(dynamical systems)方法对宇宙学演化进行综合分析。首先建立幽灵场模型的一般性质并扩展Heusler命题。针对五类不同的势函数,在三种各向异性时空背景(Bianchi I、Bianchi III和Kantowski-Sachs)上构建动力系统。通过选择适当的动力学变量,系统地识别临界点(critical points),并分析其稳定性和物理意义。整个分析过程强调数学结果的物理解释,并详细阐述每个数学工具和变量选择的理论依据。

Result: 在三种各向异性时空背景和五类势函数的组合下,共识别出87个临界点,并对每个临界点的性质进行了详细分析。这些临界点对应不同的宇宙学演化阶段,包括暴涨解、各向同性化过程以及各种渐近行为。分析结果揭示了幽灵场模型中暴涨和各向同性化的丰富动力学结构,展示了不同势函数和时空几何如何影响宇宙演化的长期行为。研究发现某些临界点对应稳定的暴涨吸引子,而另一些则表现出复杂的过渡动力学。

Conclusion: 本文通过系统的动力系统分析,全面揭示了幽灵场模型在各向异性时空中的暴涨动力学和各向同性化机制。扩展的Heusler命题与动力系统方法相结合,克服了传统分析的局限性。对87个临界点的分析表明,各向异性初始条件下的宇宙演化具有丰富的相空间结构。Collins-Hawking和Burd-Barrow的经典结论在幽灵场框架下得到重新审视,揭示了其适用范围和需要修正的方面。研究提出了若干具有深刻概念意义的问题,为理解早期宇宙的各向异性演化和暴涨机制提供了新的理论视角,也为幽灵场宇宙学和各向异性暴涨理论的进一步研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate inflationary dynamics and isotropization in a phantom-field model with five families of potentials on Bianchi I, Bianchi III, and Kantowski-Sachs backgrounds. First, the general properties of the model are established, and Heusler's proposition is extended. To address the limitations of this proposition, a powerful dynamical-systems approach is then adopted to provide a comprehensive analysis of the cosmological evolution. In total, 87 critical points are identified and their properties are analyzed. Throughout the analysis, we emphasize the physical interpretation of the mathematical results and aim to provide a thorough explanation of the rationale behind the choice of each mathematical tool and variable. Within this framework, the conclusions of Collins and Hawking and of Burd and Barrow are revisited, and several challenging questions with deep conceptual implications are posed and discussed.

</details>


### [116] [Global Integrated Null Energy Contribution : Classification of Traversable Wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39468)
*Soumya Chakrabarti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究可穿越虫洞喉部的零能量条件局部违反问题，证明体积积分的零能量贡献可以为正值，并基于此重构了一类虫洞几何，提出了基于零能量条件全局体积积分测度的新分类方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿越虫洞喉部的局部零能量条件违反被普遍认为意味着负能量，但现有研究仅证明总体积积分零能量可以任意接近零。本文旨在挑战这一传统认知，探索体积积分零能量贡献是否可以为正值，并据此建立更完善的虫洞分类体系。

Method: 通过理论分析证明虫洞喉部局部违反零能量条件时，总体积积分的零能量贡献可以为正值；利用该论证重构可穿越虫洞几何的数学模型；基于零能量条件的全局体积积分测度，建立虫洞的新分类框架。

Result: 成功证明了体积积分零能量贡献可以为正值，突破了之前"任意接近零"的结论；重构出一族满足该条件的可穿越虫洞几何；建立了基于全局体积积分测度的虫洞分类方案。

Conclusion: 可穿越虫洞喉部的局部零能量条件违反不必然导致总体积积分零能量为负，该积分量甚至可以为正值。基于零能量条件全局体积积分测度的新分类方法为研究虫洞物理提供了新的理论视角，拓展了对虫洞几何性质的理解。

Abstract: We argue that the local violation of null energy condition at the throat of a traversable wormhole does not necessarily indicate that the total volume integrated null energy contribution is negative. It is already known that it can be arbitrarily close to zero. We show that it can even be positive and use this argument to reconstruct a family of wormhole geometries. We also propose a novel classification of traversable wormholes based on the global volume integrated measure of null energy condition.

</details>


### [117] [Conserved currents associated with conformal symmetry in linearized gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39476)
*Gabor Zsolt Toth*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在闵可夫斯基背景下研究线性化引力的守恒流,系统构建了与洛伦兹变换、标度变换和特殊共形变换相关的守恒张量体系,并推广了诺特定理以处理规范固定条件下的对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已建立了线性化引力场的能量-动量张量理论,但缺乏对完整时空对称性(特别是共形对称性)对应守恒流的系统研究。需要在拉格朗日场论框架下构建与洛伦兹、标度和特殊共形变换相关的守恒流,并处理规范固定条件带来的技术难题。

Method: 1) 借鉴线性化引力与电动力学的相似性,构造特殊的角动量张量M_lg^abc、标度流D_lg^a和能量-动量张量T_lg^ab; 2) 推广诺特定理以适用于附加规范固定条件的情形; 3) 证明特殊共形变换是广义调和规范下线性化引力的诺特对称性; 4) 利用推广的诺特定理导出特殊共形张量C_lg^ab; 5) 构建包含正则能量-动量张量的多参数张量族,导出存在物质时的局部平衡方程。

Result: 成功构建了线性化引力的完整守恒流体系:Distinguished张量T_lg^ab及其配套的M_lg^abc、D_lg^a和C_lg^ab;确定了伴随一般能量-动量张量族的多参数M^abc、D^a和C^ab张量族;所有张量成员仅依赖于线性化度规的一阶导数;推导了存在物质源时相关的局部平衡方程;证明了广义调和规范下特殊共形变换的诺特对称性。

Conclusion: 本文在拉格朗日场论框架下系统完成了线性化引力守恒流的构建,通过推广诺特定理解决了规范固定条件下的对称性问题,建立了包含能量-动量、角动量、标度流和共形张量的完整体系。这些结果为理解线性化引力的对称性结构提供了理论基础,所得张量族仅涉及度规一阶导数,具有良好的数学性质。

Abstract: Extending a previous work on the energy-momentum tensor of the linearized gravitational field in Minkowski background, conserved currents in linearized gravity associated with Lorentz transformations, dilatations and special conformal transformations are explored in the framework of Lagrangian field theory. Both outstanding special cases and general parametric families of currents are considered. Regarding the former, the distinguished energy-momentum tensor $T_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$ we found earlier, guided by similarities between linearized gravity and electrodynamics, is supplemented with an angular momentum tensor $M_{\mathrm{lg}}^{abc}$ and a dilatation current $D_{\mathrm{lg}}^a$, associated with Lorentz and dilatation symmetries. Concerning special conformal transformations, a generalization of Noether's theorem that is useful if additional conditions (such as gauge fixing conditions) are imposed is formulated, it is shown that special conformal transformations are Noether symmetries of linearized gravity in the generalized harmonic gauges, and a special conformal tensor $C_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$, accompanying $T_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$, is obtained using the generalized Noether's theorem. The angular momentum tensor, dilatation current and special conformal tensor accompanying two other notable energy-momentum tensors are discussed as well. General multi-parameter families of $M^{abc}$, $D^a$ and $C^{ab}$ tensors accompanying a general family of energy-momentum tensors, which was found earlier and includes canonical energy-momentum tensors, are also determined, and local balance equations that are relevant if matter is present are derived for them. Their members do not depend on higher than first derivatives of the linearized metric.

</details>


### [118] [Phenomenology of dynamical trapped regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39543)
*Julio Arrechea,Stefano Liberati,Hooman Neshat,Vania Vellucci*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了动态演化的黑洞模拟天体（BHM）和正则黑洞（RBH）的现象学后果，通过数值演化时间依赖Hayward类几何上的线性标量扰动，发现晚期响应信号保留了内部动力学历史的记忆效应，而非仅由最终构型决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典黑洞的替代方案——正则黑洞和无视界黑洞模拟天体通常被作为静态解研究，但其内部可能存在不稳定性或正在经历弛豫过程。现有研究忽略了这种动态演化过程对观测信号的影响，因此需要系统研究时间依赖几何中的扰动演化及其可观测后果。

Method: 采用穿透视界的Painlevé-Gullstrand坐标系，在预设的时间依赖Hayward类几何上数值演化线性标量扰动。考察BHM-RBH-BHM演化历史，其中最初无视界的天体临时形成捕获区后又回到无视界构型（通过单次或多次反弹）。追踪扰动穿过捕获视界形成和消失的过程，计算产生的波形和标量场能量。

Result: 对于具有相同初末态几何的演化历史，改变中间正则黑洞阶段的持续时间会导致晚期信号（包括其回声）的振幅和相位产生差异。较长的捕获相持续时间产生更大的能量放大效应，这与内捕获视界附近外向模式的蓝移一致。

Conclusion: 动态弛豫中的黑洞模拟天体的晚期响应不仅由其最终构型决定，还保留了内部动力学历史的记忆。这表明即使最终状态相同，不同的内部演化路径也会在可观测信号中留下可区分的印记，为探测这类紧致天体的内部动力学提供了新的观测途径。

Abstract: Regular black holes (RBHs) and horizonless black hole mimickers (BHMs) are often studied as stationary alternatives to classical black holes, although their interiors may be unstable or undergoing relaxation. We investigate the phenomenological consequences of this evolution by numerically evolving linear scalar perturbations on prescribed, time-dependent Hayward-like geometries. We consider BHM--RBH--BHM histories in which an initially horizonless object temporarily develops a trapped region before returning to a horizonless configuration, through either a single bounce or a sequence of repeated bounces. Using horizon-penetrating Painlevé--Gullstrand coordinates, we follow the perturbations through the formation and disappearance of trapping horizons and compute the resulting waveforms and scalar-field energy. For histories with identical initial and final geometries, we find that changing the duration of the intermediate regular black hole phase produces differences in the amplitude and phase of the late-time signal, including its echoes. Longer trapped phases also yield greater energy amplification, consistent with the blueshift of outgoing modes near the inner trapping horizon. These results show that the late-time response of a dynamically relaxing BHM need not be determined by its final configuration alone: it can retain a memory of the dynamical history of its interior.

</details>


### [119] [MADGRAV: a multilevel anomaly-detection pipeline for gravitational-wave searches applied to LIGO data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39583)
*Gianluca Inguglia,Huw Haigh,Ulyana Dupletsa,Alessandro Longo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文展示了基于深度学习的MADGRAV流水线在LIGO第三次和第四次观测运行数据中搜索大质量致密双星并合的结果，该流水线使用一系列卷积神经网络进行异常检测和信号识别，成功探测到47个引力波事件，证明了异常检测方法可作为大质量高信噪比区域匹配滤波的独立互补探测通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的引力波探测主要依赖匹配滤波方法，但对于大质量致密双星并合事件，需要开发独立的探测通道作为补充。深度学习异常检测方法有潜力在高质量、高信噪比区域提供有效的替代或互补探测手段，因此研究者开发MADGRAV流水线以验证这一方法的有效性。

Method: MADGRAV流水线由一系列顺序连接的卷积神经网络组成，包括异常检测、glitch分类、相干性测试和信号排序四个阶段。该方法对LIGO Hanford和Livingston探测器的数据进行分析，使用1秒Q变换时频窗口分别处理单个探测器数据和相干数据。候选事件需要通过所有阶段的筛选，达到显著性阈值（假警报率<1年^-1，天体物理起源概率p_astro>0.9）才被确认为引力波探测。

Result: 在通过所有流水线阶段的候选事件中，48个达到显著性阈值，报告了47个引力波探测事件。其中44个与最小模型化的相干WaveBurst搜索结果一致。探测到的源坐标系总质量范围为14-236太阳质量，中位数为69太阳质量，中位信噪比为16。对于网络信噪比>10的事件，流水线在30太阳质量以下恢复了8.1%的目录事件，30-100太阳质量之间恢复了39.8%，100太阳质量以上恢复了53.3%，显示出随质量增加的探测效率提升趋势。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基于深度学习的异常检测流水线可以作为独立的探测通道，在大质量、高信噪比区域与传统匹配滤波方法形成有效互补。MADGRAV流水线在高质量区域表现出较好的探测能力，特别是对于质量超过100太阳质量的系统恢复率超过50%，证明了深度学习方法在引力波探测领域的应用前景和实用价值。

Abstract: We present the results of \textbf{MADGRAV}, a deep-learning-based search for high-mass compact binary coalescences, applied to the data collected by the LIGO interferometers during the third observing run and during the first and second part of the fourth observing run. The \textbf{MADGRAV} pipeline consists of a series of sequential convolutional neural networks that perform anomaly detection, glitch classification, coherence testing, and signal ranking. Data from the Hanford and Livingston LIGO detectors are studied (both individually and in coherence) by way of 1 second Q-transform windows. Of the candidates that survive every stage of the pipeline, 48 reach the significance threshold, and we report 47 gravitational wave detections characterised by a false alarm rate below $1\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$ with a probability of astrophysical origin $p_{\rm astro}>0.9$. Of the 47 detections, 44 are shared with the minimally modelled coherent WaveBurst search. The observed total source-frame masses, extracted from official gravitational wave transient catalogues, are in the $14-236 M_{\odot}$ range with a median of $69 M_{\odot}$, and a median SNR of 16. We note that the recovered fraction of confident detections rises with mass: for LIGO detectors network SNR $>10$ the pipeline recovers $8.1\%$ of confident catalog events below $30 M_{\odot}$, $39.8\%$ between $30$ and $100 M_{\odot}$, and $53.3\%$ above $100 M_{\odot}$, corresponding to $33.3\%$, $45.5\%$ and $53.3\%$ of the events detected by coherent WaveBurst in the same bins. These results suggest that anomaly detection pipelines can serve as an independent detection channel complementary to matched filtering in the high-mass high-SNR regime.

</details>


### [120] [Complete total-transmission modes of Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39609)
*Changkai Chen,Xiaohua Zhang,Zhoujian Cao,Jiliang Jing,Sheng Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了Kerr黑洞引力全透射模式(TTMs)的完整谱,发现四个全局连续族(n_∞=1,2,3,4),修正了先前的三族分类,揭示了这些模式在不同自旋参数下的渐近行为,并发现了n_∞=3族TTM与非常规Kerr准正规模序列之间的异常接近现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前Cook和Lu的研究仅给出了Kerr黑洞TTMs的三族分类,且在轴对称扰动情况下提出了泛音-多重态分裂的解释。本研究旨在构建更完整、统一的TTM谱分类体系,澄清复共轭对称性与镜像对称性(连接m和-m谱)的区别,并重新审视轴对称情况下的分支演化行为,同时探索高精度数值计算下TTM与准正规模之间可能存在的关联。

Method: 通过高精度数值计算方法系统构建Kerr黑洞的TTM完整谱,计算范围扩展至ℓ=32。将复共轭对称性与m↔-m镜像对称性分离处理,建立统一的四族分类方案。分析各族在小自旋极限和大|aω|极限下的渐近行为,研究球对称极限下的极标记与大角动量排序之间的族依赖关系。针对轴对称扰动(m=0)情况,追踪分支在例外点的合并及其向小自旋极限的演化。通过高精度数值分辨,识别n_∞=3 TTM族与非常规Kerr准正规模序列之间的异常接近现象。

Result: 成功构建了四个全局连续的TTM族(n_∞=1,2,3,4),提供了比Cook-Lu三族分类更统一的框架。发现n_∞=3族趋向Schwarzschild代数特殊频率,而n_∞=1,2,4族沿下半平面方向(-150°,-90°,-30°)以ω∝a^(-4/3)发散。高精度数据验证了Cook-Lu的小自旋渐近结果,并展示了发散分支如何嵌入全局四族谱中。对于轴对称扰动,证实n_∞=2和n_∞=3分支在例外点合并后演化至不同的小自旋极限,而非Cook-Lu提出的泛音-多重态分裂模式。识别出n_∞=3 TTM族与非常规Kerr准正规模序列之间存在分离度达10^(-8)量级的异常接近,该现象在数值上得到充分分辨。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了Kerr黑洞引力全透射模式的完整四族谱分类体系,澄清了对称性结构,修正了先前关于轴对称扰动分支演化的解释。发现的n_∞=3 TTM族与非常规准正规模之间的异常接近现象揭示了Kerr时空中不同模式家族之间可能存在的深层联系,为理解黑洞扰动理论的全局结构提供了新视角。高精度数值结果为后续理论分析和引力波物理应用奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We construct the complete spectrum of gravitational total-transmission modes (TTMs) of Kerr black holes and find four globally continuous families, $n_\infty=1,2,3,4$. Compared with the three-family classification of Cook and Lu [Phys. Rev. D 107, 044043 (2023)], our global continuation separates complex conjugation at fixed $m$ from the mirror symmetry connecting the $m$ and $-m$ spectra, yielding a uniform four-family classification without additional symmetry-unrelated roots. The $n_\infty=3$ family approaches the Schwarzschild algebraically special frequency, whereas the $n_\infty=1,2,$ and $4$ families diverge as $ω\propto a^{-4/3}$ along lower-half-plane directions $-150^\circ$, $-90^\circ$, and $-30^\circ$. High-precision data up to $\ell=32$ recover the Cook--Lu small-spin asymptotics and show how the divergent branches are embedded in the global four-family spectrum. The spherical-limit polar labeling and large-$|aω|$ angular ordering are related in a family-dependent manner. For axisymmetric perturbations, we show that the $n_\infty=2$ and $n_\infty=3$ branches coalesce at exceptional points and subsequently evolve toward distinct small-spin limits, rather than exhibiting the overtone-multiplet splitting proposed by Cook and Lu. Finally, we identify a previously unreported anomalous proximity between the $n_\infty=3$ TTM family and the unconventional Kerr quasinormal-mode sequence, with separations reaching order $10^{-8}$ while remaining numerically well resolved.

</details>


### [121] [Neutrino Oscillations without Mass: A Re-analysis of KamLAND Data following the Dirac Equation in Curved Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39638)
*Golam Mortuza Hossain,Pushpit Kumar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于弯曲时空中的狄拉克方程，提出了一种新的中微子振荡模型，将无质量中微子的味态视为具有能量依赖能级分裂的二能级量子态，并通过重新分析KamLAND数据集证明该模型比标准的有质量中微子范式具有更高的拟合似然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的中微子振荡理论基于有质量中微子范式，但在弯曲时空中，狄拉克方程表明引力对能量和质量的影响不同，需要对传播态的不变质量和守恒能量进行区分处理。这促使研究者探索一种替代模型来描述中微子振荡现象。

Method: 将双味中微子振荡的味态建模为无质量中微子的二能级量子态系统，其能级分裂依赖于能量，类似于修正的Jaynes-Cummings模型。使用该理论框架重新分析公开发布的KamLAND实验数据集，并与标准有质量中微子模型进行似然度比较。

Result: 基于修正Jaynes-Cummings模型的无质量中微子二能级量子态模型对KamLAND观测数据的拟合似然度高于标准的有质量中微子范式，表明该替代模型能够更好地描述实验数据。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在考虑弯曲时空效应时，将中微子振荡描述为能量依赖的二能级量子系统可能比传统的有质量中微子模型更符合实验观测。这为理解中微子物理提供了一种新的理论视角，挑战了标准的有质量中微子振荡范式。

Abstract: The Dirac equation in stationary curved spacetime implies that describing two-flavor neutrino oscillations requires treating the invariant mass and conserved energy of each propagating state distinctly, as gravity affects energy differently than mass. By re-analysing the publicly released KamLAND dataset, we show that modeling flavor states of massless neutrinos as two-level quantum states with energy-dependent level splitting, analogous to modified Jaynes-Cummings models, results in a higher-likelihood fit to the observed data than the standard massive-neutrino paradigm.

</details>


### [122] [Spherical thin shells with charge in unimodular gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39664)
*Gabriel R. Bengochea,Ernesto F. Eiroa,Griselda Figueroa-Aguirre*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在单模引力理论框架下构造了球对称薄壳结构,分析了其在径向对称性保持扰动下的动力学稳定性,并构建了带电真空泡模型(内部闵可夫斯基时空、带电薄壳、外部单模引力几何)。研究表明这些时空避免了事件视界和中心奇点,并发现其稳定性区域和物质含量与广义相对论的对应结果存在由单模引力非守恒参数控制的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单模引力(UG)理论框架下研究薄壳结构的动力学性质,特别是探索如何利用UG的非守恒特性构造无事件视界和中心奇点的新型时空解(如带电真空泡),并与广义相对论中的对应结果进行比较,以揭示UG理论的独特物理效应。

Method: 1) 应用单模引力的结合条件构造球对称薄壳结构;2) 采用由径向电场源产生的非守恒带电外部解;3) 构建带电真空泡模型:内部闵可夫斯基时空通过带电薄壳与外部UG几何匹配;4) 对薄壳在径向、保对称性扰动下进行动力学稳定性分析;5) 将稳定性区域和物质含量与广义相对论的结果进行对比。

Result: 成功构造了无事件视界和中心奇点的带电真空泡时空(在类德西特情况下,壳层外可能存在宇宙学视界);稳定性分析表明,单模引力中的稳定性区域和薄壳物质含量与广义相对论存在差异,这些差异由UG的非守恒参数控制;研究扩展了先前已发表的相关结果。

Conclusion: 单模引力理论允许构造具有特殊性质的薄壳时空结构,特别是可以避免事件视界和中心奇点的带电真空泡解。UG的非守恒参数在决定系统的动力学稳定性和物质分布方面起关键作用,导致与广义相对论预测的可观测差异。这为检验单模引力理论提供了可能的观测窗口,并丰富了对引力理论中薄壳结构的理解。

Abstract: We construct spherically symmetric thin shells in unimodular gravity (UG) and analyze their dynamical stability under radial, symmetry-preserving perturbations. We use the UG junction conditions and adopt non-conservative charged exterior solutions sourced by a radial electric field. As an application, we build charged vacuum bubbles: a Minkowski interior surrounded by a charged thin shell and matched to an exterior UG geometry. The resulting spacetimes are free of event horizons and central singularities, while in de Sitter-like cases a cosmological horizon may exist outside the shell. We compare the stability regions and the matter content with their counterparts in general relativity, finding some differences controlled by the UG non-conservation parameter. This work extends previously published results.

</details>


### [123] [Poisson spot and wave scattering of scalar fields by conformal anomaly black holes: probing the near-horizon geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39672)
*Zeming Zhuang,Hongsheng Zhang,Zong-Kuan Guo,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量平面波在静态共形反常黑洞中的散射和衍射现象，通过部分波级数方法计算了完整波形，揭示了清晰的衍射图样（包括泊松亮斑和衍射环），分析了吸收截面和微分散射截面，发现近视界几何对泊松亮斑强度最为敏感，电荷和共形反常参数对glory散射峰产生相反的宽度效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标量平面波在共形反常黑洞时空中的散射和衍射行为，探索共形反常修正如何影响黑洞的光学特性和散射截面，特别是近视界几何结构对衍射图样（如泊松亮斑）和散射特征的影响，以深入理解量子引力修正对黑洞物理的贡献。

Method: 采用部分波级数（PWS）方法在有限距离处计算完整波形，确定截断条件；通过分析度规函数和势垒结构研究吸收截面；使用级数约化方法加速PWS收敛以计算微分散射截面；系统分析泊松亮斑的轴向强度随径向坐标的变化，对比共形反常黑洞与RN黑洞的差异；研究电荷参数和共形反常参数对glory散射的影响。

Result: 波形展现清晰的衍射图样：负z轴上场退化为入射平面波，正z轴上扭曲平面波与散射球面波共存，θ=0处出现泊松亮斑并被同心衍射环包围；泊松亮斑强度对近视界几何最敏感，近视界处与RN黑洞显著不同，远场则趋于一致；不同的f'(r_+)值产生不同相位增量，压缩和平移干涉条纹，共形反常修正以O(α̃M²/r⁴)衰减；低频和高频吸收截面分别与势垒的ℓ=0部分和ℓ依赖部分强相关；微分截面和glory散射主要源于排除外视界小邻域的有限区域散射；电荷和α̃对glory峰宽度有相反效应，对峰高有相同效应。

Conclusion: 静态共形反常黑洞的散射和衍射特性受近视界几何和共形反常修正的显著影响。泊松亮斑强度对视界附近的度规敏感，度规函数的导数f'(r_+)控制干涉图样，而共形反常修正在远场快速衰减。吸收截面在不同频率下由势垒的不同部分主导，glory散射主要发生在远离视界的有限区域。电荷和共形反常参数对散射特征产生可区分的效应，为通过观测区分不同黑洞模型提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the scattering and diffraction of scalar plane waves by static conformal anomaly black holes. We determine the truncation requirements for the partial-wave series (PWS) method at finite distances and compute the full waveforms, which display a clear diffraction pattern: on the negative $z$-axis the field reduces to the incident plane wave, while on the positive $z$-axis a distorted plane wave coexists with a scattered spherical wave, and a bright Poisson spot appears at $θ=0$ surrounded by concentric diffraction rings. We obtain the on-axis intensity of the Poisson spot as a function of the radial coordinate. The intensity is most sensitive to the near-horizon geometry: close to the horizon it differs markedly from that of the RN black hole, whereas in the far field the two curves converge. We trace this to the metric functions, where different values of $f'(r_+)$ produce different phase increments that compress and shift the interference fringes, while the conformal anomaly correction falls off as $O(\tildeαM^2/r^4)$. By analyzing the potential barrier we study the absorption cross section, and use the series reduction method to accelerate the PWS convergence for the differential scattering cross section. The low- and high-frequency absorption cross sections are strongly correlated with the $\ell=0$ and $\ell$-dependent parts of the potential barrier, respectively. The differential cross section and glory scattering arise mainly from scattering in a finite region excluding a small neighborhood of the outer horizon. Charge and $\tildeα$ have opposite effects on the width of the glory peak but the same effect on its height.

</details>


### [124] [Gravitational Memory: an approximate description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39694)
*Q. -L. Zhao,P. -M. Zhang,M. Elbistan,J. Balog,P. A. Horvathy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了三明治型引力波在大距离极限下的行为，提出可用连续但非光滑的轮廓函数来近似描述粒子运动，该近似模型与Carroll对称性一致，并通过Poschl-Teller轮廓函数进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三明治型引力波的精确描述在大距离情况下过于复杂，需要发展简化的近似模型来描述粒子在引力波中的运动行为，同时确保该模型与底层的对称性结构（Carroll对称性）保持一致。

Method: 采用连续但非必然光滑的轮廓函数来近似描述三明治型引力波的大距离行为，通过保持与Carroll对称性的一致性来构建简化模型，并选取Poschl-Teller轮廓函数作为具体实例来演示该方法的有效性。

Result: 建立了一个与Carroll对称性相容的近似模型，该模型能够简化描述粒子在三明治型引力波中的运动。通过Poschl-Teller轮廓函数的实例验证了该近似方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 三明治型引力波在大距离极限下可以用连续非光滑轮廓进行有效近似，该近似框架与Carroll对称性相容，为研究引力波中的粒子动力学提供了简化且理论自洽的描述方法。Poschl-Teller轮廓成功展示了该策略的应用。

Abstract: The large-distance behaviour of a sandwich gravitational wave can be approximated by a continuous but not necessarily smooth profile, providing us with a simplified description of particle motion. Our approximate model is consistent with the Carroll symmetry. Our strategy is illustrated by the Poschl-Teller profile.

</details>

Method: 采用对称性分析方法对拉格朗日密度为$f_1(φ)R+f_2(φ,X)$形式的k-essence模型进行分类。关键技术包括：(1)通过拉格朗日乘子将动能项X引入为独立自由度；(2)将lapse函数视为动力学变量而非在作用量层面固定；(3)在任何规范固定之前对约束系统进行对称性分析；(4)利用发现的对称性构造Noether守恒律；(5)基于守恒律求解场方程获得精确宇宙学解。

Result: 研究识别出两大类具有非平凡对称性的k-essence模型族：最小耦合和非最小耦合引力理论。利用相应的Noether守恒律成功导出了精确宇宙学解。发现当守恒荷的数值为零时，场方程简化为代数关系。在此基础上推导出标度因子的幂律表达式，其指数由特定的k-essence函数决定。这些结果提供了新的精确解类别，并揭示了对称性与宇宙学演化之间的深层联系。

Conclusion: 通过在规范固定前对约束系统进行对称性分析，本研究揭示了k-essence模型中原本会被传统方法遗漏的非平凡对称性。这种方法不仅完成了对空间平坦FLRW背景下k-essence模型的系统分类，还通过Noether守恒律获得了新的精确宇宙学解。特别是发现守恒荷为零时场方程的代数化简以及相应的幂律标度因子解，为理解修正引力理论中的宇宙学动力学提供了新的理论工具和物理洞察。

Abstract: In the context of a spatially flat FLRW background, we perform a symmetry classification of $k$-essence models with Lagrangian densities of the form $f_1(φ)R+f_2(φ,X)$. The kinetic term $X$ is introduced as an independent degree of freedom via a Lagrange multiplier, and the lapse function is treated as a dynamical variable. The symmetry analysis is applied to the constrained system prior to any gauge fixing. This approach reveals symmetries which otherwise are lost when the lapse is fixed at the level of the action. The derived families of $k$-essence models admitting nontrivial symmetries fall to two general classes: minimally coupled and nonminimally coupled theories to gravity. We use the corresponding Noetherian conservation laws to derive exact cosmological solutions. We find that, when the numerical value of the conserved charges is zero, the field equations reduce to an algebraic relation. We subsequently derive power-law expressions for the scale factor with exponents determined by the particular $k$-essence function.

</details>


### [126] [Testing charged bumblebee black holes through high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations in X-ray binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39864)
*Qi-Qi Liang,De-Jiang Yin,Zi-Qiang Cai,Zheng-Wen Long*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 1) 分析静态球对称带电bumblebee黑洞时空中零测地线和类时圆轨道,计算光子球半径、最内稳定圆轨道(ISCO)半径和特征轨道频率;2) 在相对论性进动模型框架下,建立HFQPO双峰频率与基本参数M、X=r/M、l₁、l₂和Q₀/M的关系;3) 引入有效参数集Θ=(M,X,C,β)处理参数简并问题;4) 使用贝叶斯MCMC方法,利用三个黑洞X射线双星(GRO J1655-40、XTE J1550-564和GRS 1915+105)的HFQPO观测数据进行参数推断。

Result: 对三个黑洞X射线双星系统的分析显示,复合参数C的68%置信区间均包含Reissner-Nordström极限C=1,表明在当前模型假设下未检测到统计显著的净洛伦兹破缺修正。由于不同的参数组合(l₁,l₂,Q₀/M)可产生相同的QPO预测,仅凭HFQPO观测无法区分洛伦兹破缺效应和电荷相关效应,存在本质性的参数简并。

Conclusion: 目前的HFQPO观测数据与标准Reissner-Nordström黑洞预测一致,未发现洛伦兹对称性破缺的明显证据。由于洛伦兹破缺参数与电荷参数在频率表达式中以组合形式出现,导致参数简并,单纯依靠HFQPO观测无法独立约束这些基本物理量。未来需要更高精度、更多类型的观测数据才能有效区分和约束洛伦兹破缺效应与电荷效应。

Abstract: We investigate strong-field orbital dynamics for static spherically symmetric charged bumblebee black holes featuring spontaneous Lorentz-violating effects. Analysis of null and timelike circular geodesics yields the photon-sphere radius, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) radius, and characteristic orbital frequencies for test particles. Within the relativistic precession model for high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations (HFQPOs), observed twin-peak frequencies are governed by the full set of fundamental parameters $M$, $X=r/M$, $l_1$, $l_2$, and $Q_0/M$. Among them, $l_1$, $l_2$, and $Q_0/M$ enter frequency expressions only in combined forms, giving rise to intrinsic parameter degeneracy. An effective-parameter set $Θ=(M,X,C,β)$ is accordingly introduced to characterize these composite contributions. Bayesian MCMC parameter inference is carried out using HFQPO observational data from three black-hole X-ray binaries: GRO J1655--40, XTE J1550--564, and GRS 1915+105. The 68\% credible intervals of composite parameter $C$ all contain the Reissner--Nordström limit $C=1$, revealing no statistically significant net Lorentz-violating correction under our model assumptions. Distinct triples $(l_1,l_2,Q_0/M)$ yield identical QPO predictions, so HFQPO observations alone cannot disentangle Lorentz-violating and charge-related effects; precise constraints on these fundamental quantities require additional, more precise observational data.

</details>


### [127] [Zero modes and oscillatory instabilities of a Lorentz-violating Kalb-Ramond field on a Schwarzschild background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39936)

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了固定Schwarzschild背景下具有四次对称破缺势和非最小Riemann耦合的Lorentz破缺Kalb-Ramond场的平衡构型和线性扰动，发现电单极响应在简并构型附近呈现代数增强而非动力学不稳定性，并揭示了高阶多极子的零频模塔结构和偶极子的振荡不稳定性转变机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Lorentz对称性破缺在引力背景下的物理效应，特别是探索Kalb-Ramond场在Schwarzschild黑洞时空中的平衡态和稳定性问题，这对理解量子引力效应、弦论低能有效理论以及修改引力理论中的场论动力学具有重要意义。

Method: 采用线性扰动理论分析方法：(1)在固定Schwarzschild度规背景上建立含四次对称破缺势和非最小Riemann耦合的Kalb-Ramond场方程；(2)对静态球对称构型进行分析，研究电分量由特征函数代数确定的性质；(3)对不同多极矩(monopole、dipole等)展开模式分析，计算零频模和有限频率模的本征谱；(4)通过参数扫描研究耦合常数变化时模态的合并和不稳定性转变过程。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1)电单极响应在接近简并构型时呈现$|\widehat e(ω,r_c)|\propto(γ_c-γ)^{-1/2}$标度律，但传播单极子振幅保持正则，表明增强源于代数约束而非动力学不稳定；(2)获得高阶多极子的精确零频模塔：$ξ_{\ell n}=-(\ell+n+1)(\ell+n+2)/3$；(3)对于$\ell=1$偶极模，有限频谱包含两个不同的低频分支；(4)当Riemann耦合变得更负时，纯虚不稳定模合并并离开虚轴，形成$ω_\pm=\pm ω_R+iω_I$的振荡不稳定性，实部分裂在合并点附近遵循平方根定律。

Conclusion: 研究证实Lorentz破缺Kalb-Ramond场在Schwarzschild背景下展现出丰富的动力学行为：电单极的代数增强机制不同于动力学不稳定，多极子存在系统的零频模谱结构，且偶极子在特定参数区域经历从纯虚模到复频振荡不稳定的拓扑转变。这些结果为理解对称破缺标量-张量场在强引力场中的非平凡响应提供了理论基础，对修改引力理论和黑洞扰动理论具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: We study equilibrium configurations and linear perturbations of a Lorentz-violating Kalb--Ramond field with a quartic symmetry-breaking potential and a nonminimal Riemann coupling on a fixed Schwarzschild background. For static spherical configurations, the electric component is determined algebraically by a characteristic function that can develop a finite-radius double root. Approaching this degenerate configuration, the monopole electric response scales as $|\widehat e(ω,r_c)|\propto(γ_c-γ)^{-1/2}$, while the propagating monopole amplitude remains regular, showing that the enhancement originates from the algebraic constraint rather than from a dynamical instability. For higher multipoles, we obtain an exact tower of zero-frequency modes, $ξ_{\ell n}=-(\ell+n+1)(\ell+n+2)/3$. For $\ell=1$, the finite-frequency spectrum contains two distinct low-frequency branches. As the Riemann coupling becomes more negative, the corresponding purely imaginary unstable modes coalesce and leave the imaginary axis as $ω_\pm=\pmω_R+iω_I$, producing an oscillatory instability. Near the merger, the real-part splitting follows a square-root law.

</details>


### [128] [On the topology of the space of vacuum initial data sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39987)
*Romain Gicquaud,Jonathan Glöckle*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了闭流形上真空初始数据集空间具有许多非平凡同伦群,通过参数化共形方法将满足严格主导能量条件的初始数据集空间中的非平凡同伦元素推广到真空初始数据集空间,这是关于该空间全局拓扑的首个结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然第二作者已构造出满足严格主导能量条件的初始数据集空间的非平凡同伦群元素,且与Bernd Ammann的联合工作表明这些元素在去除严格性假设后仍然存在,但尚无研究探讨这些元素能否在真空初始数据集空间中表示。本文旨在填补这一空白,研究真空初始数据集空间的全局拓扑性质。

Method: 采用参数化版本的共形方法(parametrized conformal method)作为主要技术工具。关键技术包括:(1)证明从度量空间中移除具有共形Killing向量的度量不改变其弱同伦型;(2)在剩余度量上建立York分解的族化版本,证明TT-张量构成平凡Hilbert丛。通过这些技术,将已知的非平凡同伦元素映射到真空初始数据集空间。

Result: 成功证明了闭流形上真空初始数据集空间包含许多非平凡同伦群。具体而言,满足严格主导能量条件的初始数据集空间中的非平凡同伦元素可以由映射到真空初始数据集空间来表示。获得了两个具有独立价值的技术结果:具有共形Killing向量的度量可从度量空间中移除而不改变弱同伦型;York分解可在度量族上实施且TT-张量形成平凡Hilbert丛。

Conclusion: 本研究首次揭示了真空初始数据集空间的全局拓扑结构,证明该空间具有丰富的非平凡同伦群。通过参数化共形方法和相关技术工具,成功将已知的非平凡拓扑结构从满足主导能量条件的初始数据集空间扩展到真空情形,为广义相对论中初始数据集空间的拓扑研究提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: We show that the space of vacuum initial data sets on a closed manifold often has many non-trivial homotopy groups. The starting point is a result of the second named author, which constructs non-trivial elements in the homotopy groups of the space of initial data sets satisfying the strict dominant energy condition, together with a later result in joint work with Bernd Ammann showing that these elements often persist when the strictness assumption is dropped. In this work, we use a parametrized version of the conformal method to show that these elements may also be represented by maps into the space of vacuum initial data sets. This requires two results that may be of independent interest: metrics admitting conformal Killing vectors can be removed from the space of metrics without changing its weak homotopy type, and over the remaining metrics the York decomposition can be carried out in families, the TT-tensors forming a trivial Hilbert bundle. To our knowledge, this is the first result on the global topology of this space.

</details>


### [129] [Projective Symmetry and Its Breaking in Quadratic Metric-Affine Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39988)
*Carmen Ferrara,María José Guzmán,Laur Järv*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究四维宇称偶数度规-仿射引力中的广义投影对称性及其破缺，证明完全投影不变性将12个引力系数约化为5参数族，并揭示了投影对称性在统一识别仿射规范模式、约束物质耦合以及关联引力对称破缺与有效物质相互作用方面的核心作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度规-仿射引力理论包含挠率和非度规性，具有丰富的几何自由度和耦合参数空间。需要系统研究广义投影对称性如何约束理论参数、简化场方程，以及该对称性与物质场耦合的关系，特别是投影对称性如何作为统一原理识别规范自由度、约束物质耦合并连接对称破缺与物质有效相互作用。

Method: 首先推导广义投影变换在仿射几何不可约分量上的作用，确定轴投影、度规迹投影和完全投影不变理论的耦合关系。针对投影不变性导致的联络算符奇异性，发展对称适配的真空场方程求解方法。然后系统分析Dirac场、电磁场和复Klein-Gordon场的耦合，研究局域精确投影变换与物质对称性的对应关系，以及通过补偿Stückelberg场保持双重对称性的机制。最后分析精确投影不变性对联络动力学的限制和显式破缺的物理效应。

Result: 完全投影不变性将12个引力耦合系数约化为5参数族，并在联络方程中产生4个矢量Noether恒等式。局域精确轴投影变换在经典层面对应无质量费米子的手征旋转，而协矢量组合作为阿贝尔联络将局域精确矢量投影代表与标量U(1)对称性关联。标准Maxwell理论独立于仿射联络，但挠率依赖的类Maxwell扩展可通过补偿Stückelberg场同时保持电磁和投影不变性。精确引力投影不变性使相应联络方向非动力学化，并强制源它们的物质流消失；受控显式破缺提升这些零模并将其转化为辅助场。

Conclusion: 广义投影对称性为度规-仿射引力理论提供了统一的约束原理：它显著减少理论参数空间，确定联络场方程的奇异结构，建立引力几何与物质场对称性之间的深层联系（如手征对称、U(1)规范对称），并揭示对称破缺如何系统地将非动力学规范模式转化为介导有效物质相互作用的辅助场，为理解扩展引力理论中的规范自由度和物质耦合提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate generalized projective symmetry and its breaking in four-dimensional parity-even metric-affine gravity, considering an action linear in curvature and at most quadratic in torsion and nonmetricity. We derive the action of the generalized projective transformation on the irreducible components of the affine geometry and determine the coupling relations defining the axial, metric-trace, and fully projectively invariant theories. Complete invariance reduces the $12$ gravitational coefficients to a five-parameter family and generates four vectorial Noether identities for the connection equations. Projective invariance makes the connection operator singular, thus we develop a symmetry-adapted method for solving its vacuum field equations. We then analyze couplings to Dirac, electromagnetic, and complex Klein-Gordon fields. Locally exact axial-projective transformations correspond to chiral rotations for massless fermions at the classical level, while a combination of covectors acts as an Abelian connection linking a locally exact vector-projective representative to scalar $U(1)$ symmetry. Standard Maxwell theory is independent of the affine connection, whereas a torsion-dependent Maxwell-like extension can preserve both electromagnetic and projective invariance through compensating Stückelberg fields. Exact gravitational projective invariance makes the corresponding connection directions nondynamical and forces matter currents sourcing them to vanish. Controlled explicit breaking lifts these zero modes and converts them into auxiliary fields. Thus, projective symmetry provides a unified principle for identifying affine gauge modes, constraining matter couplings, and relating symmetry breaking in the gravitational sector to effective matter interactions.

</details>


### [130] [Axion corrections to photon superradiant scattering by Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40166)
*Zhi-Qing Zhu,Jun Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在轴子-光子耦合存在的情况下,克尔黑洞的光子超辐射散射现象,并探讨了这种耦合如何改变电磁放大因子。研究发现在辐射区和亚阈值区两种不同物理机制下,轴子场对光子放大的影响截然不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的克尔黑洞超辐射散射理论未考虑轴子-光子耦合效应。轴子作为暗物质候选粒子,其与光子的相互作用可能显著改变黑洞周围的电磁放大过程。因此需要在完全相对论框架下研究耦合系统,以理解轴子场如何调制光子超辐射现象,这对理解黑洞物理和暗物质性质具有重要意义。

Method: 采用完全相对论框架求解耦合的轴子场和电磁场方程。根据激发轴子频率与轴子质量的关系,将物理过程划分为两个区间:辐射区(radiative regime)和亚阈值区(subthreshold regime)。通过数值求解耦合场方程,计算不同参数空间下的电磁放大因子,分析轴子准束缚态激发对能量传输的影响。

Result: 研究发现两种截然不同的物理机制:(1)在辐射区,轴子可以传播到空间无穷远并带走能量,从而抑制光子放大效应;(2)在亚阈值区,轴子被质量势垒束缚,通过准束缚态激发与黑洞视界交换能量。在亚阈值区,光子放大可以被增强或抑制,具体取决于相关轴子模式是否满足超辐射条件。

Conclusion: 轴子-光子耦合显著改变了克尔黑洞的光子超辐射散射特性。耦合效应的具体表现依赖于轴子质量与激发频率的相对关系:辐射区总是抑制放大,而亚阈值区的效应由轴子模式的超辐射条件决定。这一研究为利用黑洞超辐射现象探测轴子暗物质提供了理论基础,并揭示了黑洞物理与基本粒子物理交叉领域的新现象。

Abstract: We study photon superradiant scattering by a Kerr black hole in the presence of an axion-photon coupling and investigate how this coupling modifies the electromagnetic amplification factor. We solve the coupled axion and electromagnetic equations in a fully relativistic framework. We distinguish two regimes according to the relation between the sourced axion frequency and the axion mass. In the radiative regime, the axion can propagate to spatial infinity and carry away energy, thereby suppressing photon amplification. In the subthreshold regime, the axion is confined by the mass barrier and exchanges energy with the black-hole horizon through quasibound-state excitations. In this regime, photon amplification can be either enhanced or suppressed, depending on whether the relevant axion modes satisfy the superradiance condition.

</details>


### [131] [Slowly rotating Black Holes in DHOST Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17614)
*Hugo Candan,Karim Noui,David Langlois*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了退化高阶标量张量(DHOST)理论中的慢旋转黑洞解，采用Hartle-Thorne近似方法，证明了参考系拖曳函数ω的微分方程在任何DHOST理论中都是可积的，并获得了其显式形式，同时分析了旋转对最内稳定圆轨道(ISCO)和光子圆轨道的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DHOST理论是广义相对论的重要推广，但目前对该理论框架下旋转黑洞解的研究尚不充分。为了更全面地理解DHOST理论中的黑洞性质及其与广义相对论的差异，需要系统研究慢旋转黑洞解的构造方法和物理特性，特别是旋转如何影响黑洞周围的轨道结构和光传播行为。

Method: 采用Hartle-Thorne近似方法处理慢旋转黑洞问题：从DHOST理论的静态球对称度规解出发，引入小旋转参数展开；推导并求解控制参考系拖曳函数ω的微分方程，证明其在任何DHOST理论中的可积性；分析ω函数的角度依赖性，通过视界和无穷远处的正则性条件约束其形式；将该方法应用于具有初级毛发的黑洞解，计算旋转对赤道面内轨道结构的影响。

Result: 成功证明了在任何DHOST理论中，参考系拖曳函数ω(仅依赖于径向坐标)所满足的微分方程都是可积的,并获得了其显式解析形式。证明了与广义相对论类似，视界和无穷远处的正则性条件禁止ω函数具有角度依赖性。通过将该形式应用于具有初级毛发的黑洞解,详细分析了旋转对最内稳定圆轨道(ISCO)位置和赤道面内圆形光子轨道的具体影响。

Conclusion: 本文建立了在DHOST理论框架下系统构造慢旋转黑洞解的通用方法，证明了参考系拖曳函数的可积性是该理论的普适性质。研究表明DHOST理论中的慢旋转黑洞在某些方面(如角度依赖性的禁止)与广义相对论表现出相似性，但旋转会对具有标量毛发的黑洞周围的轨道结构产生显著影响，为通过天体物理观测区分DHOST理论与广义相对论提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study slowly rotating black hole solutions within Degenerate Higher Order Scalar Tensor (DHOST) theories. Starting from a static, spherically symmetric metric solution of a DHOST theory, we employ the Hartle-Thorne ansatz to model a slowly rotating spacetime. We show that the differential equation governing the frame-dragging function $ω$ (which is supposed to depend on the radial coordinate only) is integrable for any DHOST theory allowing us to obtain its explicit form. We also consider angular dependence in $ω$ and show that regularity at the horizon and at infinity forbids it, as in General Relativity. As an illustration of the formalism introduced here, we study the slowly-rotating version of black hole solutions with primary hair obtained recently, examining the influence of the rotation on the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO) and on the circular light trajectories in the equatorial plane.

</details>


### [132] [Flat limit of artificial cosmology for scalar waves in Schwarzschild-de Sitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40243)
*Anıl Zenginoğlu,Govind Arun Kumar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在小宇宙常数情况下,宇宙学波形如何近似孤立系统的波形,通过Schwarzschild-de Sitter背景上的标量波进行定量分析,验证了平直极限的数值表现,并扩展了Misner的人工宇宙学方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于两方面:(1)观测到的宇宙常数数值很小;(2)Misner提出用共形紧化方法来正规化Einstein方程中形式上的奇异项。需要定量研究当宇宙常数很小时,宇宙学波形如何过渡到孤立系统的渐近平直波形。

Method: 使用Schwarzschild-de Sitter背景上的标量波进行定量研究。采用桥接叶层(bridge foliations)从黑洞视界延伸到宇宙学视界,确保叶层在未来共形边界保持正则。在几何匹配的数据上对比宇宙学波形和渐近平直波形。扩展Misner的人工宇宙学方法,引入不改变近区几何的宇宙学源项。

Result: 平直极限在数值上表现良好,能够恢复渐近平直波形的基本特征。引入宇宙学源项后,改善了铃宕(ringdown)波形的一致性,但未能准确捕捉中间多项式尾部行为。宇宙学波形与渐近平直波形在几何匹配数据上的对比验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究证实了在小宇宙常数极限下,宇宙学波形能够数值良好地近似孤立系统的渐近平直波形,恢复其主要特征。扩展的Misner人工宇宙学方法虽然改进了铃宕阶段的一致性,但对于完整描述波形演化(特别是多项式尾部行为)仍存在局限性。该工作为理解宇宙学背景下的引力波信号及其平直时空极限提供了定量基础。

Abstract: How do cosmological waveforms approximate waveforms from isolated systems for a small cosmological constant? This question is motivated by the observed small value of the cosmological constant, and also by Misner's idea to regulate the formally singular terms introduced into the Einstein equations by conformal compactification. We investigate this question quantitatively using scalar waves on Schwarzschild--de Sitter backgrounds. A suitable flat limit requires the foliation to remain regular at the future conformal boundary. We compare bridge foliations extending from the black hole horizon to the cosmological horizon with a regular asymptotically flat limit. Comparisons of cosmological and asymptotically flat waveforms on geometrically matched data show that the flat limit is numerically well behaved and recovers the essential features of the asymptotically flat waveform. We extend Misner's artificial cosmology by introducing a cosmological source that leaves the near-zone geometry unchanged and show that it improves agreement of the ringdown waveform but fails to capture the intermediate polynomial tail behavior accurately.

</details>
